   275	    #[Route(path: '/%eccube_admin_route%/content/block/{id}/delete', name: 'admin_content_block_delete', requirements: ['id' => '\d+'], methods: ['DELETE'])]
   276	    public function delete(Request $request, Block $Block, Filesystem $fs, CacheUtil $cacheUtil): RedirectResponse
   277	    {
   278	        $this->isTokenValid();
   279	
   280	        // ユーザーが作ったブロックのみ削除する
   281	        if ($Block->isDeletable()) {
   282	            $dir = sprintf('%s/app/template/%s/Block',
   283	                $this->getParameter('kernel.project_dir'),
   284	                $this->getParameter('eccube.theme'));
   285	
   286	            $fileName = $Block->getFileName();
   287	            $filesToDelete = [
   288	                $fileName.'.ja.twig',
   289	                $fileName.'.en.twig',
   290	            ];
   291	
   292	            foreach ($filesToDelete as $twigFile) {
   293	                $file = $dir.'/'.$twigFile;
   294	                if ($fs->exists($file)) {
   295	                    $fs->remove($file);
   296	                }
   297	            }
   298	
   299	            $this->entityManager->remove($Block);
   300	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   301	
   302	            $event = new EventArgs(
   303	                [
   304	                    'Block' => $Block,
   305	                ],
   306	                $request
   307	            );
   308	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CONTENT_BLOCK_DELETE_COMPLETE);
   309	
   310	            $adapter = $this->fileManager->getAdapter();
   311	            $contentBucketOptions = $this->fileManager->getContentBucketOptions();
   312	            foreach ($filesToDelete as $twigFile) {
   313	                if ($adapter->exists($this::BLOCK_PATH.$twigFile, $contentBucketOptions)) {
   314	                    $adapter->delete($this::BLOCK_PATH.$twigFile, $contentBucketOptions);
   315	                } else {
   316	                    log_error($this::BLOCK_PATH.$twigFile.' はS3に存在しません');
   317	                }
   318	            }
   319	
   320	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.delete_complete', 'admin');
   321	
   322	            // キャッシュの削除
   323	            $cacheUtil->clearTwigCache();
   324	            $cacheUtil->clearDoctrineCache();
   325	        }
   326	
   327	        return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_content_block');
   328	    }
   329	}

   152	
   153	        if ($tagId !== null) {
   154	            // 単一ID(例: 1)/複数ID(例: 1-2-3)に関わらず、パスの {tagId} を tags[] 配列へ正規化する。
   155	            // ここで用意したタグ配列がユニサーチパラメータの fq.tag に implode されて送信される。
   156	            $request->query->set('tags', array_values(array_filter(explode('-', $tagId), static fn (string $v): bool => $v !== '')));
   157	        }
   158

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	/*
     4	 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
     5	 *

     7	 *
     8	 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/

    13	
    14	namespace Eccube\EventListener;
    15	
    16	use Eccube\Exception\App\BaseApiException;
    17	use Eccube\Exception\CloudFileAccessedBeforeMergeException;
    18	use Eccube\Request\Context;
    19	use Eccube\Service\App\DeckBuilder\CorsHeaderApplier;
    20	use Symfony\Component\EventDispatcher\EventSubscriberInterface;
    21	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\JsonResponse;
    22	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
    23	use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Event\ExceptionEvent;
    24	use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\HttpExceptionInterface;
    25	use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\KernelEvents;
    26	use Twig\Environment;
    27	
    28	class ExceptionListener implements EventSubscriberInterface
    29	{
    30	    /**
    31	     * ExceptionListener constructor.
    32	     */
    33	    public function __construct(
    34	        private readonly Environment $twig,
    35	        protected Context $requestContext,
    36	        private readonly CorsHeaderApplier $corsHeaderApplier,
    37	    ) {
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public function onKernelException(ExceptionEvent $event): void
    41	    {
    42	        $title = trans('exception.error_title');
    43	        $message = trans('exception.error_message');
    44	        $statusCode = Response::HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    45	
    46	        $exception = $event->getThrowable();
    47	
    48	        /**
    49	         * スマレジ Webhook の場合は JSON でエラーを返す（API と同様）
    50	         */
    51	        if ($this->requestContext->isSmaregiWebhook()) {
    52	            $statusCode = $exception instanceof HttpExceptionInterface
    53	                ? $exception->getStatusCode()
    54	                : Response::HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    55	            $message = $exception instanceof HttpExceptionInterface && $exception->getMessage()
    56	                ? $exception->getMessage()
    57	                : 'An error occurred';
    58	            log_error('Smaregi webhook error', [
    59	                'status' => $statusCode,
    60	                'message' => $exception->getMessage(),
    61	                'file' => $exception->getFile(),
    62	                'line' => $exception->getLine(),
    63	                'trace' => $exception->getTraceAsString(),
    64	            ]);
    65	            $event->setResponse(new JsonResponse([
    66	                'status' => 'error',
    67	                'message' => $statusCode >= 500 ? 'Internal server error' : $message,
    68	            ], $statusCode));
    69	
    70	            return;
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        /**
    74	         * APIの場合は、エラー詳細をログ出力し、JSON形式で必ず(code, errors)を返す
    75	         */
    76	        if ($this->requestContext->isAppApi()) {
    77	            $logLevel = 'error';
    78	            $errors = ['システムエラーが発生しました'];
    79	            $statusCode = Response::HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    80	
    81	            $logException = $exception->getPrevious() ?: $exception;
    82	            $errorClass = (new \ReflectionClass($exception))->getShortName();
    83	
    84	            // カスタムエラーの場合は、エラーコードとメッセージを取得
    85	            if ($exception instanceof BaseApiException) {
    86	                $statusCode = $exception->getStatusCode();
    87	                $errors = $exception->getErrors();
    88	                $logLevel = $exception->getLogLevel();
    89	            // HttpException は statusCode を尊重（4xxはwarning, 5xxはerror）
    90	            } elseif ($exception instanceof HttpExceptionInterface) {
    91	                $statusCode = $exception->getStatusCode();
    92	                $logLevel = $statusCode >= 500 ? 'error' : 'warning';
    93	
    94	                // 返却メッセージのポリシー:
    95	                // - 401/403 は固定文言（漏洩防止）
    96	                // - 400/404/422 は元メッセージを返す
    97	                // - それ以外は汎用文言
    98	                $errors = match ($statusCode) {
    99	                    Response::HTTP_BAD_REQUEST => [$exception->getMessage() ?: 'リクエストが不正です'],
   100	                    Response::HTTP_NOT_FOUND => [$exception->getMessage() ?: 'リソースが見つかりません'],
   101	                    Response::HTTP_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY => [$exception->getMessage() ?: '入力値が不正です'],
   102	                    Response::HTTP_UNAUTHORIZED => ['認証エラー'],
   103	                    Response::HTTP_FORBIDDEN => ['権限エラー'],
   104	                    default => ['システムエラーが発生しました'],
   105	                };
   106	            }
   107	
   108	            // ログ出力
   109	            $logContext = [
   110	                'status' => $statusCode,
   111	                'title' => $title,
   112	                'message' => $message,
   113	                'errors' => $errors,
   114	                'line' => $logException->getLine(),
   115	                'level' => $logLevel,
   116	            ];
   117	            switch ($logLevel) {
   118	                case 'error':
   119	                    $logContext += [
   120	                        'method' => $this->requestContext->getMainRequest()?->getMethod(),
   121	                        'file' => $logException->getFile(),
   122	                        'trace' => $logException->getTraceAsString(),
   123	                    ];
   124	                    log_error($errorClass, $logContext);
   125	                    break;
   126	                case 'warning':
   127	                    log_warning($errorClass, $logContext);
   128	                    break;
   129	                case 'info':
   130	                    log_info($errorClass, $logContext);
   131	                    break;
   132	                case 'debug':
   133	                    log_debug($errorClass, $logContext);
   134	                    break;
   135	                default:
   136	                    log_error('Unknown LogLevel: '.$logLevel, $logContext);
   137	                    break;
   138	            }
   139	
   140	            $response = new JsonResponse([
   141	                'code' => $statusCode,
   142	                'errors' => $errors,
   143	            ], $statusCode);
   144	
   145	            $request = $event->getRequest();
   146	            $route = $request->attributes->get('_route');
   147	            if (is_string($route) && str_starts_with($route, 'api_deck_builder_')) {
   148	                $this->corsHeaderApplier->apply($response, $request->headers->get('Origin'));
   149	            }
   150	
   151	            $event->setResponse($response);
   152	
   153	            return;
   154	        }
   155	
   156	        if ($exception instanceof CloudFileAccessedBeforeMergeException) {
   157	            $infoMess = 'クラウドサービスからのファイルが環境に追加される前にアクセスされました。';
   158	            $title = trans('exception.error_title_s3_file_accessed_before_merge');
   159	            $message = trans('exception.error_message_s3_file_accessed_before_merge');
   160	        } elseif ($exception instanceof HttpExceptionInterface) {
   161	            $statusCode = $exception->getStatusCode();
   162	            switch ($statusCode) {
   163	                case 400:
   164	                case 401:
   165	                case 403:
   166	                case 405:
   167	                case 406:
   168	                    $infoMess = 'アクセスできません。';
   169	                    $title = trans('exception.error_title_can_not_access');
   170	                    if ($exception->getMessage()) {
   171	                        $message = $exception->getMessage();
   172	                    } else {
   173	                        $message = trans('exception.error_message_can_not_access');
   174	                    }
   175	                    break;
   176	                case 429:
   177	                    $infoMess = '試行回数の制限を超過しました。';
   178	                    $title = trans('exception.error_title_can_not_access');
   179	                    $message = trans('exception.error_message_rate_limit');
   180	                    break;
   181	                case 404:
   182	                    $infoMess = 'ページがみつかりません。';
   183	                    $title = trans('exception.error_title_not_found');
   184	                    $message = trans('exception.error_message_not_found');
   185	                    break;
   186	                default:
   187	                    break;
   188	            }
   189	        }
   190	
   191	        if (isset($infoMess)) {
   192	            log_info($infoMess, [
   193	                $exception->getMessage(),
   194	                $exception->getFile(),
   195	                $exception->getLine(),
   196	            ]);
   197	        } else {
   198	            log_error('システムエラーが発生しました。', [
   199	                $exception->getMessage(),
   200	                $exception->getFile(),
   201	                $exception->getLine(),
   202	                $exception->getTraceAsString(),
   203	            ]);
   204	        }
   205	
   206	        try {
   207	            $file = $this->requestContext->isAdmin() ? '@admin/error.twig' : 'error.twig';
   208	            $content = $this->twig->render($file, [
   209	                'error_title' => $title,
   210	                'error_message' => $message,
   211	            ]);
   212	        } catch (\Exception) {
   213	            $content = $title;
   214	        }
   215	
   216	        $event->setResponse(new Response($content, $statusCode));
   217	    }
   218	
   219	    /**
   220	     * Returns an array of event names this subscriber wants to listen to.
   221	     *
   222	     * The array keys are event names and the value can be:
   223	     *
   224	     *  * The method name to call (priority defaults to 0)
   225	     *  * An array composed of the method name to call and the priority
   226	     *  * An array of arrays composed of the method names to call and respective
   227	     *    priorities, or 0 if unset
   228	     *
   229	     * For instance:
   230	     *
   231	     *  * array('eventName' => 'methodName')
   232	     *  * array('eventName' => array('methodName', $priority))
   233	     *  * array('eventName' => array(array('methodName1', $priority), array('methodName2')))
   234	     *
   235	     * @return array<string, array<int, string>> The event names to listen to
   236	     */
   237	    #[\Override]
   238	    public static function getSubscribedEvents(): array
   239	    {
   240	        return [
   241	            KernelEvents::EXCEPTION => ['onKernelException'],
   242	        ];
   243	    }
   244	}
   100	            return;
   101	        }
   102	
   103	        $route = $this->getRoute($event->getRequest());
   104	        $this->logger->debug('LOGIC END', [$route]);
   105	    }
   106	
   107	    public function onKernelTerminate(TerminateEvent $event): void
   108	    {
   109	        $route = $this->getRoute($event->getRequest());
   110	        $this->logger->debug('PROCESS END', [$route]);
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    public function onKernelException(ExceptionEvent $event): void
   114	    {
   115	        $e = $event->getThrowable();
   116	        if ($e instanceof HttpExceptionInterface && $e->getStatusCode() < 500) {
   117	            $this->logger->info($e->getMessage(), [$e->getStatusCode()]);
   118	        } else {
   119	            $message = sprintf(
   120	                '%s: %s (uncaught exception) at %s line %s',
   121	                $e::class,
   122	                $e->getMessage(),
   123	                $e->getFile(),
   124	                $e->getLine()
   125	            );
   126	            $this->logger->error($message, ['exception' => $e]);
   127	        }
   128	    }
